# Skills · S · part 12

894 entries, scene-brief to schema-query-mismatch. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **scene-brief**: Produce a single self-contained scene brief — exactly the context an LLM needs to draft the named scene as prose, and nothing else. Given a file containing the scene outline, plus optional set of supplementary context files, extracts only the material that will affect what gets written. Triggers include "scene brief", "brief the scene", "prep a scene". ([sandervw/Time-of-Dying](https://github.com/sandervw/Time-of-Dying/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/scene-brief))

- **scene-builder**: Builds strong scenes: character objective, escalating conflict, costly outcome, reaction, dilemma, decision. Spatial anchoring, subtext, rhythm, irreversibility. Use to write a scene, repair a flat one, or verify that a scene changes the state of the narrative. ([Handsomeboy990/craft-suite](https://github.com/Handsomeboy990/craft-suite/tree/HEAD/plugins/writing/skills/scene-builder) · 2 copies)
- **scene-check**: Run Scene Doctor to diagnose and fix scene issues ([pedronaugusto/blender-weave](https://github.com/pedronaugusto/blender-weave/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/scene-check) · ★8)
- **scene-composite**: Place a real person (or object) into a real photograph so it looks like they were there. Use whenever someone asks to "put me in this photo", "add this person to this scene", "composite these two images", "make it look like I was at this event", "insert this product into this room", or supplies a portrait plus a background photo and wants them merged. Covers the four geometry failures that ruin composites — floating subject, missing shadow, wrong scale, cropped head — with the exact prompt clauses that prevent each. Also covers aspect-ratio matching, reference-image order, and EXIF orientatio… ([guiloureiromkt/agent-skills](https://github.com/guiloureiromkt/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/scene-composite))

- **scene-consistency-two-cases**: 小場景切圖，大場景弱化細節。解決：換角度生成同一個場景，場景就變了；圖生圖跟抽卡都很累。 當使用者問到這類問題、或提到「場景一致性」時使用此 skill。⚠️ 技巧內容為影片作者自述（E6），我方未實測，不得作為效果承諾依據。 ([firekou/aitokenking_mediahouse](https://github.com/firekou/aitokenking_mediahouse/tree/HEAD/skills/scene-consistency-two-cases) · ★2)

- **scene-de-crime**: Chaque erreur de code est traitée comme une scène de crime. Claude est l’investigation criminelle : sécurisation du périmètre, collecte de preuves, balistique, profilage du suspect, réquisitoire formel. Fondé sur l’absurde procédural de Kafka (Le Procès, 1925) : la machine judiciaire ne distingue pas entre un homicide et un TypeError. Le même protocole criminalistique s’applique à tout. Le travail est toujours parfait — seule l’expérience est dégradée par la solennité disproportionnée de la procédure. Se déclenche dès qu’un bogue, une erreur, un avertissement ou un comportement inattendu appa… ([bacoco/useless-skills](https://github.com/bacoco/useless-skills/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/scene-de-crime) · ★2)

- **scene-detect-mcp**: Извлечение кадра на каждую смену сцены через PySceneDetect ContentDetector (HSV-взвешенный детект склеек), точнее ffmpeg select=gt(scene,X). Триггеры: 'раскадровка видео', 'смена сцены', 'кадр при смене сцены', 'scene detection', 'извлечь ключевые кадры', 'PySceneDetect', 'storyboard frames'. НЕ для кадров по таймкоду/интервалу — используй ffmpeg -vf fps=N. НЕ для YouTube-метаданных/транскриптов — используй youtube-mcp. ([Alex1980Alex/1C-Framework](https://github.com/Alex1980Alex/1C-Framework/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/scene-detect-mcp))

- **scene-ppt**: 场景化讲解 PPT（图片版，一页一张 16:9 图）。读一篇文章或一个主题，把整套内容放进你的默认「世界」里讲（小课堂、后厨、RPG、像素小岛、复古广告…第一次看样张选），逐页设计场景、定好图上每个字，交给 Codex 内置 imagegen 原样出图。当用户说「做成 PPT」「出一套讲解幻灯片」「用 PPT 讲 XX」「把这篇文章做成 PPT」「换默认世界」时触发。 ([yunshu0909/yunshu_skillshub](https://github.com/yunshu0909/yunshu_skillshub/tree/HEAD/visualization/scene-ppt) · ★760)

Use before scene attendance or specialist deployment; not for tactical entry or hazardous-device handling. ([rohasnagpal/forensic-ai-skills](https://github.com/rohasnagpal/forensic-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/forensic-ai-skills/skills/scene-readiness-planner) · ★2)
- **scene-ready-check**: Run the scene gate on a glTF 2.0 file: structure validity (buffers/accessors consistent), triangle budget vs declared target platform, true scale in meters, collision geometry present. Trigger: "is this scene game-ready", "check my glTF", "validate this level". NOT a performance profiler and NOT an art review — materials, draw calls, and taste live in the engine. ([wjlgatech/design-anything](https://github.com/wjlgatech/design-anything/tree/HEAD/skills/scene-ready-check) · ★1)
- **scene-recast-video**: Remake a known scene with a new cast: give a video model a short reference clip (a film scene, a dance, a fight, a news shot, a meme) plus photos of your own characters and a "replace, keep, never" prompt, and get your version with the motion, timing, camera and cuts of the original. Covers trimming the clip, character references, the recast prompt, new dialogue, the first-frame lock, the motion-only plate (greyscale or depth), three varg routes (Seedance 2.5 with a video reference, MiniMax H3 Max Recast, Wan 3), a frame-by-frame check against the source and a stacked original-vs-remake expor… ([vargHQ/skills](https://github.com/vargHQ/skills/tree/HEAD/scene-recast-video) · ★21)
